Ask your agent to investigate it with REA. It can inspect the app without its source code, explain how the feature works, show the evidence, and build a version for your project.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eREA connects your agent to tools for inspecting native binaries, JavaScript and Electron apps, .NET assemblies, and websites. You can also use the same tools from your terminal. Analysis runs locally, and results include the evidence and limitations behind each conclusion.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSetup registers REA with your agent and installs matching workflow instructions. Native analysis can use an existing Hopper, Ghidra, IDA or Kuna installation; setup can optionally install Hopper with approval. Static JavaScript analysis needs no native analysis engine.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://rea.tools/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eVisit the REA website\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e for setup instructions, illustrated guides, and real case studies.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuick start\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-quick-start\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Quick start\" href=\"#quick-start\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOn Linux, Ghidra, JADX, firmware tool launches and static JavaScript/Electron\nanalysis require a procps-compatible \u003ccode\u003eps\u003c/code\u003e on REA's \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e for process ownership\nchecks. See\n\"Compact input schema profile\" in the MCP contracts guide.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eComplete output schemas are advertised by default. <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">REA: Reverse Engineer Anything</h1><a id="user-content-rea-reverse-engineer-anything" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: REA: Reverse Engineer Anything" href="#rea-reverse-engineer-anything"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">One MCP for reverse engineering across binaries, applications, and runtime behavior.</h3><a id="user-content-one-mcp-for-reverse-engineering-across-binaries-applications-and-runtime-behavior" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: One MCP for reverse engineering across binaries, applications, and runtime behavior." href="#one-mcp-for-reverse-engineering-across-binaries-applications-and-runtime-behavior"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<hr>
<p dir="auto">See a feature in an app that you want in your own product? Ask your agent to investigate it with REA. It can inspect the app without its source code, explain how the feature works, show the evidence, and build a version for your project.</p>
<p dir="auto">REA connects your agent to tools for inspecting native binaries, JavaScript and Electron apps, .NET assemblies, and websites. You can also use the same tools from your terminal. Analysis runs locally, and results include the evidence and limitations behind each conclusion.</p>
<p dir="auto">Setup registers REA with your agent and installs matching workflow instructions. Native analysis can use an existing Hopper, Ghidra, IDA or Kuna installation; setup can optionally install Hopper with approval. Static JavaScript analysis needs no native analysis engine.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://rea.tools/" rel="nofollow">Visit the REA website</a></strong> for setup instructions, illustrated guides, and real case studies.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">On Linux, Ghidra, JADX, firmware tool launches and static JavaScript/Electron
analysis require a procps-compatible <code>ps</code> on REA's <code>PATH</code> for process ownership
checks. Recovery steps are in <code>docs/installation.md</code> under
"Linux owned-process prerequisite".</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Set up your agent</h3><a id="user-content-set-up-your-agent" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Set up your agent" href="#set-up-your-agent"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">With Node.js and npm installed, run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx rea-agents setup"><pre>npx rea-agents setup</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Choose your agents, review the proposed changes, and approve them. Setup adds
REA's MCP server and matching workflow instructions, with backups of existing
configuration. Restart your agent afterward. Setup records the selected runtime and REA
entry script directly. If acquired through a package runner, keep its cache entry
available. Run package acquisition from a trusted directory outside the target
project. Installation and recovery details are in the setup guide below.</p>
<p dir="auto">Setup supports Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, Grok Build and
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#supported-agents">other agents</a>. See
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md">installation and setup</a> for provider configuration and
manual MCP registration and Claude Code/Codex plugin installation.</p>
<p dir="auto">MCP uses compact input schemas by default to reduce context overhead. Set
<code>REA_MCP_INPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=full</code> in your MCP registration when you need the
complete advertised field guidance and nested validation details. Both profiles
keep the full tool set and canonical server validation. See
"Compact input schema profile" in the MCP contracts guide.</p>
<p dir="auto">Complete output schemas are advertised by default. If your client caps the
complete <code>tools/list</code> frame, set <code>REA_MCP_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=compact</code> to
bound each advertised output schema, or <code>off</code> to omit output schemas entirely.
Both settings keep canonical server-side validation and complete structured
results. See "Output schemas" in the MCP contracts guide.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Ask your agent</h3><a id="user-content-ask-your-agent" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ask your agent" href="#ask-your-agent"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Understand how search works in the Notes app, show me the evidence, and build a
similar feature for my project."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Understand how search works in the Notes app, show me the evidence, and build a
similar feature for my project.
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Replace Notes with your target app and the feature you want to understand.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Use the terminal</h3><a id="user-content-use-the-terminal" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Use the terminal" href="#use-the-terminal"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Inspect an extracted JavaScript/Electron app directory or ASAR:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx -y rea-agents@latest analyze-javascript-application /absolute/path/to/app --json"><pre>npx -y rea-agents@latest analyze-javascript-application /absolute/path/to/app --json</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The result includes modules, imports, Electron boundaries and their evidence.
Replace the path with your target, such as <code>"D:/apps/example"</code> on Windows.</p>
<p dir="auto">To install the <code>rea</code> command for regular use:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install --global rea-agents
rea --help"><pre>npm install --global rea-agents
rea --help</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For native analysis, configure a provider first. See the
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/cli.md">CLI and Evidence guide</a> for native commands, provider selection,
snapshots and scripting.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Update REA</h3><a id="user-content-update-rea" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Update REA" href="#update-rea"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">REA changes quickly, and new releases include frequent bug fixes. Keep your
installation up to date.</p>
<p dir="auto">For a CLI installed with <code>npm install --global</code> or <code>bun add --global</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="rea update"><pre>rea update</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>rea --update</code> is an alias for this verified workflow and accepts the same global
output flags. Use <code>rea update</code> when combining it with other command controls.</p>
<p dir="auto">Updater commands retain at most 4 MiB of stdout and stderr combined. Oversized
metadata replies are rejected after the command exits; verbose installs continue
with bounded diagnostics. Human progress output respects terminal backpressure
and waits for submitted writes to finish.</p>
<p dir="auto">Bun global updates require Bun 1.2.17 or later. With an older Bun release,
upgrade Bun manually before running <code>rea update</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">To refresh your agent registrations and skill, run the setup command printed
by the update.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you use <code>npx</code>, update your agent setup with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx rea-agents@latest setup"><pre>npx rea-agents@latest setup</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Review the setup changes and restart your agent. For one-off CLI commands,
use <code>npx rea-agents@latest</code> followed by the command. With Bun, use
<code>bunx rea-agents@latest</code> in place of <code>npx rea-agents@latest</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How REA works</h2><a id="user-content-how-rea-works" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How REA works" href="#how-rea-works"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Your agent calls REA through MCP to inspect the target and trace relevant code.
REA returns findings with their evidence. The agent uses them to ask follow-up
questions, explain the behavior, or write and test an implementation.
CLI commands use the same workflows.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/website/public/assets/figures/rea-investigation-flow.svg"><img src="/morluto/rea/raw/main/website/public/assets/figures/rea-investigation-flow.svg" alt="REA investigation flow: your agent asks about a local target, REA inspects and traces it using analysis tools, and the agent uses the returned code, references and unknowns to explain, implement and test." style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/website/public/assets/figures/rea-investigation-flow.svg">Open the full-size figure</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a id="user-content-current-status"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What you can analyze</h2><a id="user-content-what-you-can-analyze" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What you can analyze" href="#what-you-can-analyze"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">REA requires Node.js 22.x (&gt;=22.23.4), 24.x (&gt;=24.22.1), or 26.12.1+, plus npm.
Additional tools and host support depend on the target:</p>

</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Static JavaScript and .NET inspection read the supplied files without running
the application. Runtime capture runs or interacts with the selected target
using your user permissions; each runtime guide describes its effects.</p>
<p dir="auto">For offline native-binary inspection, REA also supports caller-supplied GNU
objdump and Rizin. See the <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/reverse-engineering-tools.md">reverse-engineering tools guide</a>
for provider setup, CLI commands, and Evidence limitations. REA does not install
or update either provider.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a id="user-content-choosing-a-deep-analysis-provider"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Native formats and host support vary by provider. See
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#hopper">Hopper and Ghidra setup</a>, the
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/ida-provider.md">IDA guide</a> (including an optional native MCP bridge), the
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/kuna-provider.md">Kuna decompiler guide</a>, and
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/windows-ghidra-p0.md">experimental Windows Ghidra support</a>.
Ghidra also supports <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/ghidra-dos.md">16-bit DOS analysis</a>.
For large binaries, raise the provider's startup deadline with
<code>REA_GHIDRA_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS</code> or <code>REA_HOPPER_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS</code>.
For provider selection, see the <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/cli.md#choose-a-provider">CLI guide</a>.
Check <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#released-package-and-main">release availability</a>
for features added since the latest npm release.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">DX-Ball: reconstruct a sound-pan calculation</h3><a id="user-content-dx-ball-reconstruct-a-sound-pan-calculation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: DX-Ball: reconstruct a sound-pan calculation" href="#dx-ball-reconstruct-a-sound-pan-calculation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Follow a sound call into its position-to-pan helper, inspect the instructions,
and turn incomplete pseudocode into C. The reconstruction passes 3,205
original-x86 cases and reproduces all 63 compiled function bytes.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://rea.tools/showcase/dx-ball/" rel="nofollow">Read the case study</a> \u00b7
<a href="https://github.com/N0zoM1z0/dx-ball">Reconstruction repository</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Notion: trace the Electron clipboard bridge</h3><a id="user-content-notion-trace-the-electron-clipboard-bridge" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Notion: trace the Electron clipboard bridge" href="#notion-trace-the-electron-clipboard-bridge"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Find the renderer's clipboard API, follow it through preload and IPC into the
main process, and inspect the rich clipboard format.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://rea.tools/showcase/notion/" rel="nofollow">Read the case study</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">TH04: recover a DOS bullet-ring calculation</h3><a id="user-content-th04-recover-a-dos-bullet-ring-calculation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: TH04: recover a DOS bullet-ring calculation" href="#th04-recover-a-dos-bullet-ring-calculation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Inspect the original PC-98 game's 16-bit instructions, recover the fixed and
aimed angle calculations, and compare the reconstructed C++ with the
historical compiler output.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://rea.tools/showcase/th04/" rel="nofollow">Read the case study</a> \u00b7
<a href="https://github.com/N0zoM1z0/th04">Reconstruction repository</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Build RL environments with REA</h3><a id="user-content-build-rl-environments-with-rea" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Build RL environments with REA" href="#build-rl-environments-with-rea"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Record a web app session, turn its API into tools, and export an environment an AI agent can use. Run it with AgentEnv, then add tasks and reward checks for RL training or evaluation.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://rea.tools/guides/rl-environments/" rel="nofollow">Read the guide</a> \u00b7
<a href="https://x.com/reatools/status/2112574822584918075?s=20" rel="nofollow">Watch the video</a></p>
<p dir="auto">If you've used REA on something interesting, we'd love to see it. Share your
case in an <a href="https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues">issue</a> or a
<a href="https://github.com/morluto/rea/pulls">pull request</a>, including the target,
your question, how REA helped, and what you found.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Build RL environments</h2><a id="user-content-build-rl-environments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Build RL environments" href="#build-rl-environments"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Give an AI agent a local environment to practice tasks such as finding an issue or creating a folder, without running the original app. Use it for reinforcement learning (RL) or to test how well an agent completes tasks.</p>
<p dir="auto">REA turns recorded API requests and responses into tools the agent can call. The environment uses the recorded data and supports changes only when the recording confirms their effects.</p>
<p dir="auto">With REA set up in your agent, replace the example URL and folder tasks below with your own:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Use REA to build an RL environment from my test web app at
http://127.0.0.1:3000.
Cover listing, creating, renaming, and deleting folders. You may create and
remove test folders. Leave existing data unchanged.
Save it in a folder named my-env. Check which operations work and tell me
how to run it."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Use REA to build an RL environment from my test web app at
http://127.0.0.1:3000.
Cover listing, creating, renaming, and deleting folders. You may create and
remove test folders. Leave existing data unchanged.
Save it in a folder named my-env. Check which operations work and tell me
how to run it.
</code></pre></div>
<details>
<summary>Use the command line instead</summary>
<p dir="auto">Use these commands if you already have a file containing the app\u2019s recorded browser requests and responses. Running the result also requires AgentEnv and Docker.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="rea environment-build ./session.har ./my-env --name my-env --json
agent-env run ./my-env"><pre>rea environment-build ./session.har ./my-env --name my-env --json
agent-env run ./my-env</pre></div>
</details>
<p dir="auto">REA exports a runnable environment with a basic test to check that it starts and responds. Use it with your RL framework to define task goals, add reward checks, and run training.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://rea.tools/guides/rl-environments/" rel="nofollow">Build an environment</a> \u00b7
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/agent-environments.md">Environment reference</a> \u00b7
<a href="https://x.com/reatools/status/2112574822584918075?s=20" rel="nofollow">Watch the demo on X</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">FAQ</h2><a id="user-content-faq" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: FAQ" href="#faq"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<details>
<summary><strong>Which agents can use REA?</strong></summary>
<p dir="auto">Any agent that supports local MCP servers. Setup configures the
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#supported-agents">supported agents</a>; other clients can use
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#mcp-registry">manual MCP registration</a>.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><strong>Do I need a native analysis engine?</strong></summary>
<p dir="auto">Deep native analysis uses Hopper, Ghidra, IDA or Kuna, depending on the target
and operation. Static JavaScript and .NET inspection work without a native
analysis engine. Setup can install Hopper after approval; Ghidra, IDA and Kuna
use your existing installations. See <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#hopper">provider setup</a>
for the options and their supported targets.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><strong>Do I need to start Hopper first?</strong></summary>
<p dir="auto">REA starts Hopper when an operation needs it. On macOS, a first-run dialog may
ask you to choose demo mode or activate your license. Complete that prompt in the
same user session before your first CLI/MCP operation or unattended job. Open
Hopper manually if needed. See
<a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#launcher-paths-and-troubleshooting">Hopper startup and troubleshooting</a>.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><strong>What does installing the skill from skills.sh do?</strong></summary>
<p dir="auto">The skill supplies investigation instructions for your agent. Use <code>npx rea-agents setup</code>
to register REA's MCP server and install the matching instructions, then restart
your agent. See <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#skill-only-installation">skill-only installation</a>.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><strong>What code does REA return?</strong></summary>
<p dir="auto">Native analysis returns pseudocode and assembly. JavaScript/Electron analysis
recovers modules and their relationships. Your agent uses these findings to
write and test an implementation; the <a href="#showcases">showcases</a> give worked examples.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><strong>Does REA upload my app?</strong></summary>
<p dir="auto">REA analyzes targets locally. Your agent receives the tool results, and its
model provider has its own data policy.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><strong>What should I do if I hit a bug?</strong></summary>
<p dir="auto">Update first; a recent release may already fix it.</p>
<p dir="auto">For a CLI installed with <code>npm install --global</code> or <code>bun add --global</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="rea update"><pre>rea update</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For agent setup through <code>npx</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx rea-agents@latest setup"><pre>npx rea-agents@latest setup</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you're using an agent, complete the <a href="#update-rea">setup refresh</a> and restart
it. Retry the same task. If the problem persists, <a href="https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues">open an issue</a>
with your REA version, target type, steps to reproduce and error output.</p>
</details>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Documentation</h2><a id="user-content-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Documentation" href="#documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Start with the website's <a href="https://rea.tools/guides/" rel="nofollow">worked guides</a>.
For exact options, prerequisites and result contracts:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md">Installation and setup</a>: agent registration, provider configuration, updates and uninstall.</li>
<li><a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/installation.md#check-readiness-for-your-task">Readiness and troubleshooting</a>: diagnose one agent or analysis engine.</li>
<li><a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/cli.md">CLI and Evidence</a>: commands, provider selection, snapshots, import/export and exit statuses.</li>
<li><a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/mcp-contracts.md">MCP contracts</a> and <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/mcp-prompts.md">agent prompts</a>: tool results, sessions and guided investigations.</li>
<li><a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/mcp-contracts.md#generated-catalog">Tool catalog</a>: generated inventory of tools, providers and CLI commands.</li>
<li><a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/docs/roadmap.md">Roadmap</a>: planned work and capability trackers.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Report vulnerabilities through <a href="/morluto/rea/blob/main/SECURITY.md">SECURITY.md</a>.</p>
